GopherCRM API

Base path /api/v1 · 153 operations · 144 schemas · Swagger 2.0

GopherCRM is a self-hosted CRM: users, leads, customers, companies, deals, products, quotes, tickets, tasks, labels, follow-up automation, notifications, embeddable lead-capture forms and answer-engine visibility tracking (AEO). Every operation in this reference lives under the base path /api/v1 (configurable through API_PREFIX). GET /health is served outside the base path and is deliberately not part of this reference.

Authentication

Protected operations accept one credential in the Authorization header, either Authorization: Bearer <jwt> (an access token from POST /auth/login or POST /auth/refresh) or Authorization: ApiKey <key> (a personal key from POST /api-keys; the plaintext key is shown once, at creation, and the gcrm_ prefix may be sent or left off). No other header, query parameter or cookie is read. A missing header, or one that is neither form, is 401 UNAUTHORIZED "Missing or invalid authorization header"; an invalid or expired credential, or one whose account is deactivated or erased, is 401 UNAUTHORIZED "Invalid credentials".

Login returns an access token (valid for JWT_EXPIRY_HOURS, 24 hours by default), a single-use refresh token (valid for REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRY_DAYS, 30 days by default) and the user. POST /auth/refresh rotates strictly: the presented refresh token is spent and replaced, a spent token is refused with 401, and of several concurrent refreshes of one token exactly one succeeds, so a client must keep the replacement from every response. Logout, POST /auth/change-password and password reset revoke refresh tokens (a password changed through PUT /users/me revokes none), but an issued access token cannot be revoked early: it stays valid until it expires. Registration is open only while the security.allow_public_registration setting is on (it ships off), always creates a customer and returns an access token without a refresh token.

Roles are admin, sales, support and customer. Each operation names the roles it admits; another role gets 403 FORBIDDEN "Insufficient permissions". Where a role sees only some records (for example sales and its own leads), the operation describes the narrowing and the status a record outside it is answered with.

Response envelope

Every JSON response has the shape {success, data, error, meta}. success is true for a 2xx response; data carries the payload (its shape is documented per operation); error is present only on failures; meta always carries request_id and, on paginated lists, page, per_page, total and total_pages (zero values are omitted, so an empty page has no total). A 204 response has no body.

Exceptions that are not enveloped: GET /customers/export (text/csv), the public form pages GET /forms/public/embed.js (JavaScript), GET /forms/public/{key}/view and GET/POST /forms/public/confirm (HTML), a CORS rejection (a bare 403 with no body) and a path or method that no route matches (the router's plain-text 404 "404 page not found").

Errors

error.code is the stable value to branch on; error.message is for people and may change. Shared codes:

  • 400 VALIDATION_ERROR: a binding or field rule failed. error.details is usually an object mapping each failed field to a message; binding failures key it by the server-side Go field name (for example FirstName), while service-level field checks use the JSON name. Operations that send another shape document it (a lead conversion rule sends {field}, plus status and allowed_statuses for a refused status; POST /aeo/prompts/{id}/run with an invalid id sends a plain string), and some omit details. A body over the public form submission limit is 413 with this code.
  • 400 BAD_REQUEST: malformed JSON ("Invalid request format"), typically an unparsable path id, or another request the handler refuses as a whole.
  • 400 INVALID_INPUT and INVALID_REFERENCE: a rule the service checks beyond the binding (automation rules, bulk status requests), or an id that names no live record (for example company_id, label_ids, a quote line's product).
  • 401 UNAUTHORIZED: missing, malformed, invalid or expired credentials.
  • 403 FORBIDDEN: the role is not admitted, or the record belongs to someone else where the operation says so.
  • 404 NOT_FOUND: no live record with that id within the caller's visibility. Erased (soft-deleted) records are never returned.
  • 409 CONFLICT and SESSION_STALE: a duplicate (email, domain, label name), a refused state change, or a stale session on a password change.
  • INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION: a refused status change; 400 for a quote transition, 409 for cancelling an automation run that has already finished.
  • 429 TOO_MANY_REQUESTS: see Rate limits.
  • 500 INTERNAL_ERROR: an unexpected failure ("An unexpected error occurred"); details are logged server-side, never returned.
  • 502 MAIL_DELIVERY_FAILED (sending a quote by email), 503 SESSION_REFRESH_UNAVAILABLE (refresh), 503 PROVIDER_NOT_CONFIGURED, PROVIDER_REJECTED, PROVIDERS_UNAVAILABLE and SHUTTING_DOWN (AEO).

Each operation lists the codes it can return and the condition behind each.

Pagination, sorting and filtering

List operations take offset (default 0; negative or non-numeric values become 0) and limit (default 20; 0, negative or non-numeric values become 20; values above 100 are clamped to 100). Some lists also take a 1-based page that, when present, overrides offset. Out-of-range values are clamped or replaced, never rejected. Sortable lists accept sort_by from a fixed per-entity column list and sort_order asc or desc; what an unknown value does (ignored, defaulted or refused) is documented per operation.

Rate limits

Requests are counted per client IP (the direct peer address unless TRUSTED_PROXIES names the proxy in front) in three tiers, each with its own buckets: strict, 10 requests per minute with a burst of 5, on every unauthenticated /auth route and on public form submission and confirmation; authenticated, 120 per minute with a burst of 30, shared by every authenticated operation and counted after authentication; generous, 240 per minute with a burst of 40, on public form reads. An exceeded limit is 429 TOO_MANY_REQUESTS with error.details {"retry_after": "60s"}, a fixed hint rather than the real wait. No Retry-After or X-RateLimit-* header is sent. Preflight OPTIONS requests are not counted.

CORS and headers

Browser requests are admitted only from the configured origins (localhost development origins plus CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS), with credentials allowed, the methods GET, POST, PUT, PATCH, DELETE and OPTIONS and the request headers Origin, Content-Type, Authorization and X-Request-ID. A request from any other origin is refused with a bare 403 before any handler or log line. The public form endpoints under /forms/public answer any origin without credentials (methods GET, POST and OPTIONS, request header Content-Type).

Every response except a CORS preflight answer or a CORS rejection carries X-Request-ID: the value the client sent in that header, otherwise a generated UUID. The same value is meta.request_id in the envelope. Browsers on the configured origins can read X-Request-ID and Content-Length (exposed headers); the public form endpoints expose no headers, so a cross-origin page reads the id from meta.request_id instead.

Security schemes

NameHeaderDescription
ApiKeyAuthAuthorization (header)

Personal API key from POST /api-keys, sent in the same Authorization header as "Authorization: ApiKey <key>" (the gcrm_ prefix may be sent or left off). A header named ApiKey is not read. The plaintext key is shown only once, at creation.

BearerAuthAuthorization (header)

JWT access token from POST /auth/login or POST /auth/refresh, sent as "Authorization: Bearer <jwt>". Valid until it expires; logout does not revoke it.

auth

Login, registration, token refresh, logout, password change and reset.

POST/auth/change-password

Change the caller's password

Any authenticated role. Re-authenticates with current_password, then replaces it with new_password (at least 10 characters with an uppercase letter, a lowercase letter, a digit and a special character). The password write, the revocation of the caller's refresh tokens and the renewal of the current session are one transaction: a failure before the commit leaves the password and every session unchanged. A COMMIT whose acknowledgement fails is ambiguous (the change may have been applied) and is reported as a 500 without credentials. The login lockout counter is not touched.

With a bearer token and the client's current refresh_token in the body, that session is renewed: the token is spent, every other refresh token of the caller is revoked, and the response carries a replacement token, refresh_token and user. A refresh_token that is not a live session of the caller (unknown, revoked, expired, foreign) is 409 SESSION_STALE and nothing changes. Without a refresh_token, or for an API-key request (whose refresh_token is ignored and which never receives session credentials), every refresh token is revoked and only the message is returned.

Revocation ends renewal, not access: access tokens already issued stay valid until they expire (there is no blocklist). A wrong current password is a 400, not a 401, because the request itself is authenticated. Rate limit: the authenticated tier (120 per minute, burst 30, per client IP).

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Request: application/json · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
requestrequiredbodyhandler.ChangePasswordRequest

Current and new password, optionally the current refresh token

StatusBodyDescription
200utils.APIResponse with data: handler.ChangePasswordResponse

Password changed; refresh tokens revoked; renewed credentials when a refresh_token was presented with a bearer token

400utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

VALIDATION_ERROR: a required field is missing or new_password is shorter than 10 characters (details maps the Go field name to a message). BAD_REQUEST: malformed JSON (Invalid request format), new_password fails the complexity policy, or The current password is incorrect

401utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

UNAUTHORIZED: missing or invalid credentials, or the account was deactivated or erased during the request

409utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

SESSION_STALE: the presented refresh_token is not a live session of the caller; nothing was changed

429utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

TOO_MANY_REQUESTS: authenticated tier exceeded; details.retry_after is 60s and no Retry-After header is sent

500utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

INTERNAL_ERROR: storage failure (nothing changed when it came before the commit; a failed COMMIT is ambiguous), or a new_password over 72 bytes (bcrypt limit)

POST/auth/login

Log in and obtain session credentials

Exchanges email and password for an access token (JWT, valid JWT_EXPIRY_HOURS, 24 hours by default), a single-use refresh token for POST /auth/refresh (valid REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRY_DAYS, 30 days by default, stored server-side only as a hash) and the user. If the refresh token cannot be stored the login still succeeds without one.

Every failure is the same 401 with the message Invalid email or password: unknown email, wrong password, locked account and deactivated or erased account cannot be told apart. After 5 consecutive wrong passwords the account is locked for 15 minutes; while locked even the right password is refused, and a lapsed lock starts a fresh five-attempt window. A successful login clears the counter and stamps last_login_at (the returned user still shows the previous value).

Rate limit: the strict /auth tier, 10 requests per minute with a burst of 5 per client IP, shared by every unauthenticated /auth route.

Authentication: none (public) · Request: application/json · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
requestrequiredbodyhandler.LoginRequest

Credentials

StatusBodyDescription
200utils.APIResponse with data: handler.AuthResponse

Authenticated; token, refresh_token and user returned

400utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

VALIDATION_ERROR: email missing or not an email address, or password missing (details maps the Go field name to a message). BAD_REQUEST: malformed JSON (Invalid request format)

401utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

UNAUTHORIZED: Invalid email or password, whatever the cause (unknown email, wrong password, locked, deactivated or erased account, or a server-side failure while signing the token)

429utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

TOO_MANY_REQUESTS: strict /auth tier exceeded; details.retry_after is 60s and no Retry-After header is sent

POST/auth/logout

Log out (revoke refresh tokens)

Revokes refresh tokens of the caller (any authenticated role, bearer or API-key). With no body, an empty body or no refresh_token, every refresh token of the caller is revoked; with a refresh_token only that token is revoked, and only when it belongs to the caller. Idempotent: an unknown, already revoked or foreign token still answers 200 and discloses nothing.

Logout does not invalidate access tokens: an issued JWT stays valid until it expires (there is no blocklist), so the client must discard it. API keys are not affected.

Rate limit: the authenticated tier, 120 requests per minute with a burst of 30 per client IP, shared by every authenticated route and checked after authentication.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Request: application/json · Response: application/json

requestbodyhandler.LogoutRequest

Optional: the one refresh token to revoke

StatusBodyDescription
200utils.APIResponse with data: handler.MessageResponse

Logged out (message: Logged out successfully)

400utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

BAD_REQUEST: a non-empty body that is not valid JSON (Invalid request format)

401utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

UNAUTHORIZED: missing or malformed Authorization header, or an invalid, expired or revoked credential, or a deactivated or erased account

429utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

TOO_MANY_REQUESTS: authenticated tier exceeded; details.retry_after is 60s and no Retry-After header is sent

500utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

INTERNAL_ERROR: the refresh tokens could not be revoked

POST/auth/password-reset

Request a password reset link

Public. Always the same 200 and the same message, whether or not an account exists for the email (anti-enumeration, like login). When an active account exists, its outstanding reset tokens are invalidated, a new single-use token valid for one hour is created and a link to APP_BASE_URL/reset-password?token=... is emailed (only logged when SMTP is not configured). A deactivated account receives nothing. Storage and delivery failures are logged server-side and never change the response.

Rate limit: the strict /auth tier, 10 requests per minute with a burst of 5 per client IP, shared by every unauthenticated /auth route.

Authentication: none (public) · Request: application/json · Response: application/json

requestrequiredbodyhandler.PasswordResetRequest

Account email

StatusBodyDescription
200utils.APIResponse with data: handler.MessageResponse

Uniform acknowledgement, independent of whether the account exists

400utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

VALIDATION_ERROR: email missing or not an email address (details maps Email to a message). BAD_REQUEST: malformed JSON (Invalid request format)

429utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

TOO_MANY_REQUESTS: strict /auth tier exceeded; details.retry_after is 60s and no Retry-After header is sent

POST/auth/password-reset/confirm

Complete a password reset

Public. Spends the token from the emailed link and sets new_password, which must meet the policy (at least 10 characters with an uppercase letter, a lowercase letter, a digit and a special character). The policy is checked before the token is touched, so a weak password leaves the link usable. Tokens are single use and expire after one hour; an unknown, expired or used token, or one whose account is deactivated or erased, is the same generic 400.

In one transaction the token is claimed, the password written, the login lockout cleared and every refresh token of the account revoked. A failure before the commit rolls all of it back and the link stays usable; when the COMMIT itself fails the outcome is ambiguous (the password may have changed and the link been spent), so a retry may be refused as an invalid or expired link. Issued access tokens stay valid until they expire. Rate limit: the strict /auth tier (10 per minute, burst 5, per client IP).

Authentication: none (public) · Request: application/json · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
requestrequiredbodyhandler.PasswordResetConfirmRequest

Reset token and new password

StatusBodyDescription
200utils.APIResponse with data: handler.MessageResponse

Password reset; every refresh token revoked

400utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

VALIDATION_ERROR: token or new_password missing, or new_password shorter than 10 characters (details maps the Go field name to a message). BAD_REQUEST: malformed JSON (Invalid request format), new_password fails the complexity policy, or Invalid or expired reset token

429utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

TOO_MANY_REQUESTS: strict /auth tier exceeded; details.retry_after is 60s and no Retry-After header is sent

500utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

INTERNAL_ERROR: storage failure (before the commit: rolled back and the link stays usable; a failed COMMIT is ambiguous and the reset may have taken effect) or a new_password over 72 bytes (bcrypt limit; the token is not spent)

POST/auth/refresh

Rotate a refresh token

Strict rotation: the presented refresh token is spent and a new access token, a new refresh token and the user are returned, the same shape as login. Clients must keep the replacement from every response; presenting a spent token fails. Of several concurrent refreshes of one token exactly one succeeds.

Every rejection of the token (unknown, expired, revoked, already spent, or owned by a deactivated or erased account) is the same 401; the reason is never disclosed. A refresh whose owner turns out to be deactivated or erased also revokes the token. An operational failure (database, signing) is 503 SESSION_REFRESH_UNAVAILABLE: when it happened before the commit the rotation was rolled back and the presented token is still usable, so the client keeps it and retries; when the COMMIT itself failed the outcome is ambiguous and the retry may answer 401.

Rate limit: the strict /auth tier, 10 requests per minute with a burst of 5 per client IP, shared by every unauthenticated /auth route.

Authentication: none (public) · Request: application/json · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
requestrequiredbodyhandler.RefreshRequest

The refresh token to spend

StatusBodyDescription
200utils.APIResponse with data: handler.AuthResponse

Rotated; new token, refresh_token and user returned

400utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

VALIDATION_ERROR: refresh_token missing or empty (details maps RefreshToken to a message). BAD_REQUEST: malformed JSON (Invalid request format)

401utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

UNAUTHORIZED: Invalid or expired refresh token, whatever the reason

429utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

TOO_MANY_REQUESTS: strict /auth tier exceeded; details.retry_after is 60s and no Retry-After header is sent

503utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

SESSION_REFRESH_UNAVAILABLE: operational failure; before the commit nothing rotated and the presented token is still usable, retry later. A failed COMMIT is ambiguous and the retry may answer 401

POST/auth/register

Register a new customer account

Public self-service sign-up, open only while the security.allow_public_registration setting is true (it ships false; GET /auth/registration reports the current state). The gate fails closed: a setting that cannot be read is treated as false. The account is always created with the customer role and active; the body has no role field and nothing in it can change the role. Elevated roles come only from the admin-guarded POST /users.

On success the response carries a JWT for the new account and the user, but no refresh token: the client signs in through POST /auth/login to obtain one. Password policy: at least 10 characters with an uppercase letter, a lowercase letter, a digit and a special character (punctuation or symbol); bcrypt rejects more than 72 bytes.

Rate limit: the strict /auth tier, 10 requests per minute with a burst of 5 per client IP, one bucket shared by every unauthenticated /auth route. No side effects besides the new user row: no mail is sent and no automation event is recorded.

Authentication: none (public) · Request: application/json · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
requestrequiredbodyhandler.RegisterRequest

Registration details

StatusBodyDescription
201utils.APIResponse with data: handler.AuthResponse

Account created; token and user returned (no refresh_token)

400utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

VALIDATION_ERROR: a field failed its binding rule (details maps each Go field name, e.g. Email, to a message). BAD_REQUEST: malformed JSON (Invalid request format), the password fails the complexity policy (message names the missing class), or the user could not be stored (the underlying message is returned, e.g. a password over 72 bytes)

403utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

FORBIDDEN: public registration is disabled or the setting could not be read

409utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

CONFLICT: an account already uses this email

429utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

TOO_MANY_REQUESTS: strict /auth tier exceeded; details.retry_after is 60s and no Retry-After header is sent

500utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

INTERNAL_ERROR: the token could not be signed (the account has already been created)

GET/auth/registration

Report whether public registration is open

Public, unauthenticated probe of the security.allow_public_registration setting, so the login and sign-up screens can decide whether to offer registration (the authenticated GET /configurations/ui cannot serve a visitor without a session). A setting that cannot be read is reported as disabled, the same answer the gate on POST /auth/register enforces. Read-only, no side effects.

Rate limit: the strict /auth tier, 10 requests per minute with a burst of 5 per client IP, shared by every unauthenticated /auth route (so polling it consumes the login budget).

Authentication: none (public) · Response: application/json

StatusBodyDescription
200utils.APIResponse with data: handler.RegistrationStatusResponse

Whether POST /auth/register accepts sign-ups

429utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

TOO_MANY_REQUESTS: strict /auth tier exceeded; details.retry_after is 60s and no Retry-After header is sent

users

Staff and customer accounts, the caller's own profile, activation (admin).

GET/users

List users (admin)

Admin only (route guard). Returns a page of live accounts as a bare array in data, with the pagination in meta (page, per_page, total, total_pages; each omitted when 0). Erased accounts are not listed. Without sort_by the rows come in ascending id order; with an allowed sort_by they are ordered by that column, then by id, in the requested direction. search matches a substring (SQL LIKE) of email, first_name or last_name and combines with the sort.

Pagination: limit defaults to 20 and is capped at 100 (a missing, non-numeric or non-positive value gives 20); offset defaults to 0 (non-numeric or negative gives 0); page, when a positive integer, overrides offset with (page - 1) * limit. An unknown sort_by is ignored rather than rejected.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
pagequeryinteger

1-based page number; a positive value overrides offset

offsetqueryinteger

Number of rows to skip, ignored when page is given

default 0
limitqueryinteger

Page size; capped at 100, non-positive or invalid values give 20

default 20
sort_byquerystring

Sort column; any other value is ignored (ascending id order)

one of: "created_at", "updated_at", "email", "first_name", "last_name", "role"
sort_orderquerystring

Sort direction; any other value falls back to asc

one of: "asc", "desc"; default "asc"
searchquerystring

Substring matched against email, first_name and last_name

StatusBodyDescription
200utils.APIResponse with data: array of models.User, meta: utils.APIMeta

A page of users; pagination in meta

401utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

Create a user (admin)

Admin only (route guard). Creates an active account with the requested role, unlike the public POST /auth/register, which always creates a customer. Password policy: at least 10 characters with an uppercase letter, a lowercase letter, a digit and a special character. No mail is sent; the new user signs in with the password the admin chose.

Any authenticated role may update its own account; updating another account requires the admin role (checked before the body is read). Only non-empty fields are written. role and is_active are applied only for an admin caller and silently ignored for everyone else; unlike POST /users/{id}/deactivate, nothing here stops an admin from changing its own role or deactivating itself. A body with nothing to apply returns the account unchanged. Passwords are changed through PUT /users/me or POST /auth/change-password, not here. Changing role or is_active takes effect on the account's next request (tokens are re-checked against the stored account); no session is revoked.

DELETE/users/{id}

Erase a user (irreversible)

Admin only (route guard, repeated in the handler); an admin cannot erase its own account. This is a GDPR Article 17 erasure and cannot be undone: in one transaction every personal field is overwritten in place (the email becomes a random address in the reserved .invalid domain, the names are blanked, the password hash is made unusable, is_active becomes false and the login bookkeeping is cleared), the user's API keys, refresh tokens, password-reset tokens and notifications are deleted, the bodies of the ticket comments the user wrote become [deleted], and the row is soft-deleted. The row is kept so references from tickets, tasks, leads and deals still resolve, and the original email becomes free for a new account.

Erasing a customer-role user also erases, in the same transaction, every customer record whose user_id is that login and every lead converted into those customers. Erasing a staff user leaves the records it owns, manages or is assigned to untouched. To suspend an account reversibly use POST /users/{id}/deactivate instead.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
idrequiredpathinteger

User ID

StatusBodyDescription
204no body

Erased; no body

400utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

BAD_REQUEST: Invalid user ID, or You cannot delete your own account

401utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

UNAUTHORIZED: missing or malformed Authorization header, or an invalid, expired or revoked credential, or a deactivated or erased account

403utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

FORBIDDEN: the caller is not an admin

404utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

NOT_FOUND: no live user with this id (an already erased account included)

429utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

TOO_MANY_REQUESTS: authenticated tier exceeded (120 per minute, burst 30, per client IP); details.retry_after is 60s and no Retry-After header is sent

500utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

INTERNAL_ERROR: the erasure failed; before the commit it was rolled back and nothing was erased, while a failed COMMIT is ambiguous (the erasure may have been applied)

POST/users/{id}/deactivate

Deactivate a user (admin)

Admin only (route guard); an admin cannot deactivate its own account here. Sets is_active to false, the reversible alternative to erasure: from the next request on the account cannot log in, its bearer tokens and API keys are rejected, and a refresh of its tokens fails. No data is changed and this call deletes no refresh token, but a refresh attempted while the account is deactivated revokes the token it presented. Idempotent.

api-keys

POST/api-keys

Create an API key

Any authenticated role, through a bearer token or an existing API key; the key always belongs to the caller. The plaintext key (gcrm_ followed by 64 hexadecimal characters) is returned only in this response: only an HMAC of it is stored, so it can never be shown again. Use it as Authorization: ApiKey <key>; the gcrm_ prefix may be sent or left off. The key acts with the owner's role and stops working when it is revoked, when it expires, or when the owner is deactivated or erased.

expires_at is optional: an RFC 3339 timestamp (for example 2026-12-31T23:59:59Z) that must lie in the future; omitted or empty, the key never expires. Expiry cannot be changed afterwards. There is no limit on the number of keys per user.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Request: application/json · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
requestrequiredbodyhandler.CreateAPIKeyRequest

Key name and optional expiry

StatusBodyDescription
201utils.APIResponse with data: handler.CreateAPIKeyResponse

Key created; key holds the plaintext, shown only this once

400utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

VALIDATION_ERROR: name missing or not 3 to 100 characters (details maps Name to a message). BAD_REQUEST: malformed JSON (Invalid request format), expires_at must be an RFC3339 timestamp, or expires_at must be in the future

401utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

UNAUTHORIZED: missing or malformed Authorization header, or an invalid, expired or revoked credential, or a deactivated or erased account

429utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

TOO_MANY_REQUESTS: authenticated tier exceeded (120 per minute, burst 30, per client IP); details.retry_after is 60s and no Retry-After header is sent

500utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

INTERNAL_ERROR: the key could not be generated or stored

PUT/api-keys/{id}

Rename, revoke or reactivate an API key

Owner only (a key of another user is 403, with no admin override). Applies only the fields present: name and/or is_active. A body with neither is a 400 rather than a no-op. Setting is_active to true on a revoked key reactivates it: revocation is a flag the owner controls, not a tombstone, and the unchanged plaintext key works again. Expiry is not affected: an expired key stays unusable whatever is_active says.

GET/configurations/ui

List the UI-safe configurations

Any authenticated role. Returns, in data.configurations and in this order: every entry of the ui category, general.company_name, deals.default_currency (the currency a new deal takes when none is sent), and three synthetic read-only lead-conversion entries holding the values the convert endpoint enforces: leads.conversion.allowed_statuses (array), leads.conversion.require_notes (boolean) and leads.conversion.auto_assign_owner (boolean). The synthetic entries are not stored rows: their id is 0, their timestamps are the zero time (0001-01-01T00:00:00Z) and is_read_only is true. An entry whose setting cannot be read is left out instead of failing the request (except the ui category itself). A sensitive entry would be masked, but none of these is sensitive.

PUT/configurations/{key}

Change a configuration value (admin)

Admin only (route guard, repeated in the handler). Sets the value of an existing entry and returns it. Checks, in order: the entry exists (404), is not read-only (400), the value is in valid_values when the entry has that list (for an array entry every element must be listed), and the value matches the declared type: a string entry takes only a JSON string, a boolean entry only true or false, an integer entry only a whole number (a fraction is refused, not truncated); float, json and array entries accept any JSON value and store its JSON text. The AEO provider entries integration.aeo.{engine}_model and integration.aeo.{engine}_base_url are trimmed and checked further: a model name of at most 120 characters, a base URL that is an absolute https URL with a valid host and port and without credentials, query, fragment or zone identifier.

A sensitive entry (is_sensitive) takes the plaintext secret, stores it encrypted (AES-256-GCM, key derived from API_KEY_SECRET or JWT_SECRET) and is returned masked; send an empty string to clear it. Changes take effect on the next read of the setting, without a restart.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Request: application/json · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
keyrequiredpathstring

Configuration key, e.g. security.allow_public_registration

requestrequiredbodyhandler.SetConfigurationRequest

The new value; any JSON value except null, falsy values included

StatusBodyDescription
200utils.APIResponse with data: handler.ConfigurationResponse

The updated entry

400utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

VALIDATION_ERROR: value missing (details maps Value to a message). BAD_REQUEST: malformed JSON (Invalid request format), Configuration value cannot be null, Configuration is read-only, or Invalid value for configuration (outside valid_values, wrong type, or a rejected AEO provider setting)

401utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

UNAUTHORIZED: missing or malformed Authorization header, or an invalid, expired or revoked credential, or a deactivated or erased account

GET/dashboard/activities

Recent activity feed

Admin, sales and support (route guard; customer gets 403). Recent events the caller may see, newest first, as a bare array. There is no activity table: entries are synthesised from leads created (lead_created) and converted (lead_converted), tickets created (ticket_created) and resolved or closed (ticket_resolved), and tasks completed (task_completed).

Each source is narrowed to the caller inside its query, before its limit: lead events cover every lead for admin, the caller's own leads for sales and none for support; ticket events cover every ticket for admin and sales and the tickets assigned to the caller for support; task events cover every task for admin and the tasks assigned to the caller for sales and support. Hidden records never appear and never crowd out visible ones. Each source contributes at most limit entries; they are merged, sorted by event time descending (ties by type, then record id descending) and cut to limit. Events without a dedicated timestamp use updated_at, so a later edit moves the entry.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
limitqueryinteger

Maximum entries; values above 50 are capped at 50, missing, invalid or non-positive values give 10

default 10
StatusBodyDescription
200utils.APIResponse with data: array of handler.Activity

The feed (possibly empty)

UNAUTHORIZED: missing or malformed Authorization header, or an invalid, expired or revoked credential, or a deactivated or erased account

GET/dashboard/leads-by-status

Lead count by status

Admin, sales and support (route guard; customer gets 403). Distribution of live leads across statuses as chart data with one dataset labelled Leads. Every status is always present, in the order new, contacted, qualified, unqualified, converted, with 0 when no lead holds it; a status found in the data but unknown to the model is appended afterwards in alphabetical order rather than dropped. System-wide: not narrowed to the caller's own leads (support, which cannot read leads elsewhere, sees these counts too).

GET/dashboard/pipeline

Deal pipeline and won-this-month widgets

Admin and sales. Support passes the dashboard route guard but is refused by the handler with 403, as the customer role is by the guard. Admin sees every live deal, sales only its own. data.stages is the payload of GET /deals/pipeline without filters: all five stages in pipeline order (qualification, proposal, negotiation, won, lost), each present even when empty, with count and per-currency totals of amount_cents and weighted_cents = round_half_up(sum of amount_cents x probability / 100), rounded once per stage-and-currency total. data.won_this_month counts the deals in the won stage whose closed_at falls in the current calendar month in UTC (first instant of the month inclusive to first instant of the next exclusive), with the amounts per currency. Amounts in different currencies are never added together; totals are sorted by currency code and empty lists are [], never null.

Pipeline widgets

GET/dashboard/sales-performance

Lead conversions over time

Admin, sales and support (route guard; customer gets 403). Lead conversions over time as chart data with one dataset labelled Conversions: the number of leads in the converted status whose updated_at falls in each bucket. It counts conversions, not revenue. A lead has no converted_at column, so updated_at stands in for the conversion time and a converted lead edited later moves to the bucket of that edit. Buckets are calendar-aligned in the server's time zone and end with the one containing now: week gives the last 12 weeks starting on Monday, labelled by that Monday (2026-08-03); month the last 12 months (2026-08); quarter the last 8 quarters (2026-Q3); year the last 5 years (2026). Any other period falls back to month. System-wide: not narrowed to the caller's leads.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
periodquerystring

Bucket size and window; any other value gives month

one of: "week", "month", "quarter", "year"; default "month"

Conversions per bucket, oldest first

leads

Leads, conversion to customers, lead bulk status.

GET/leads

List leads

One page of leads. Roles: admin and sales.

Without external_id, a sales user always gets the leads it owns, in ascending id order, and search, classification, sort_by and sort_order are ignored; the owner field is not loaded on that path (all its fields are zero).

Without external_id, for an admin the parameters do not combine, the first one present wins: search (substring match on first_name, last_name, email, company, phone and notes; case-insensitive on MySQL/MariaDB with their default collations, and on SQLite for ASCII letters only; honours the sort), then classification (exact match, any value accepted, an unknown one simply matches nothing; ascending id order; owner not loaded), then sort_by, else every lead in ascending id order. An unknown sort_by is ignored silently (ascending id order) and a sort_order other than asc or desc becomes asc. Soft-deleted (erased) leads never appear.

external_id, when present and not empty, takes precedence over everything above for every role, including search, classification, sort_by, sort_order, page, offset and limit: the lookup is an SQL equality on the column, byte-exact on SQLite, while on MySQL/MariaDB the column collation decides (utf8mb4_unicode_ci in the migrations: case-insensitive, trailing spaces ignored); the value is not trimmed. external_id is not unique: the result holds every matching live lead the caller may view (admin any lead, sales only the leads it owns, every other role none), in ascending id order, and total is their number (meta.page 1, meta.per_page the parsed limit, which does not cut the list). A lead the caller may not see is left out, never 403 or 404, so a hidden lead reads like an unknown value. At most the first 100 matching leads by id are read before the visibility filter, so with more than 100 leads sharing one value later ones (including a sales user's own) are not returned. The owner is loaded on that path, company_record is not.

data is an object {leads, total}; meta carries request_id, page (offset / limit + 1, rounded down), per_page and, when not zero, total and total_pages (both are omitted for an empty result).

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
pagequeryinteger

1-based page number; when greater than 0 it overrides offset (offset = (page-1) x limit). Non-numeric values are ignored

offsetqueryinteger

Number of rows to skip; negative or non-numeric values fall back to 0

default 0
limitqueryinteger

Page size; 0, negative or non-numeric values fall back to 20, values above 100 are clamped to 100

default 20
searchquerystring

Admin only: substring searched in first_name, last_name, email, company, phone and notes

classificationquerystring

Admin only, ignored when search is present: exact classification to match

one of: "unclassified", "test", "spam", "lead", "hot_lead"
sort_byquerystring

Admin only, ignored when classification wins: sort column; any other value is ignored (no error)

one of: "created_at", "updated_at", "first_name", "last_name", "email", "company", "status", "classification", "source"
sort_orderquerystring

Sort direction for sort_by; any other value becomes asc

one of: "asc", "desc"; default "asc"
external_idquerystring

Exact external_id to look up (at most 255 characters); when present and not empty it overrides every other parameter and returns the matching leads the caller may view

max length 255

401utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

UNAUTHORIZED: the Authorization header is missing or is neither Bearer <jwt> nor ApiKey <key> ('Missing or invalid authorization header'), or the credential is invalid, expired or belongs to a deactivated account ('Invalid credentials')

POST/leads

Create a lead

Create a lead. Roles: admin and sales (the /leads group guard).

Checks, in order: the binding rules (first_name and last_name required, email well-formed, status and classification from their sets); notes at most 65535 bytes; status converted is refused (only POST /leads/{id}/convert converts); an email or a non-blank phone is required. Owner: an admin must send owner_id; a sales user may omit it (the lead becomes its own) or send its own id. company_id links a company record independently of the free-text company field: 0 or absent means no link, an id that names no live company is 400 INVALID_REFERENCE. created_at may backdate an imported lead; a value in no accepted layout is ignored and the current time is used.

Side effect: the lead_created automation event is recorded in the same transaction as the insert.

The response is the stored lead; owner is not loaded here (all its fields are zero) and company_record is omitted.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Request: application/json · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
requestrequiredbodyhandler.CreateLeadRequest

The lead to create

StatusBodyDescription
201utils.APIResponse with data: models.Lead

Lead created

400utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

VALIDATION_ERROR (message 'Validation failed'): a binding rule failed; error.details maps the Go field name (for example FirstName) to a message. BAD_REQUEST: the body is not valid JSON for the schema ('Invalid request format'). BAD_REQUEST: notes over 65535 bytes, neither email nor phone ('Provide an email address or a phone number'), or an admin without owner_id ('Owner ID is required for admin users'). VALIDATION_ERROR: status converted, with error.details {'field': 'status'}. INVALID_REFERENCE: company_id names no live company

401utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

UNAUTHORIZED: the Authorization header is missing or is neither Bearer <jwt> nor ApiKey <key> ('Missing or invalid authorization header'), or the credential is invalid, expired or belongs to a deactivated account ('Invalid credentials')

403utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

FORBIDDEN 'Insufficient permissions': the caller is not admin or sales, or a sales user sent another user's owner_id ('You can only assign leads to yourself')

429utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

TOO_MANY_REQUESTS: more than 120 requests per minute (burst 30) from this client IP, one bucket shared by every authenticated endpoint; error.details.retry_after is '60s' and no Retry-After or X-RateLimit header is sent

500utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

INTERNAL_ERROR: unexpected failure, including a database rejection (for example an owner_id that names no user, or a value longer than its column on MySQL/MariaDB)

POST/leads/bulk/status

Update the status of several leads at once

Set the same status on up to 100 leads in one all-or-nothing transaction. Roles: admin and sales (route guard; support and customers get 403 before the body is read); sales users may only update leads they own. Repeated ids are collapsed. If any listed lead is missing (404, error.details.missing_ids) or, for sales, not owned by the caller (403, error.details.forbidden_ids), nothing is written; missing ids are reported before ownership. status converted is refused with 400 VALIDATION_ERROR and error.details {field: status}: a lead becomes converted only through POST /leads/{id}/convert. Side effects: every updated lead records a lead_updated automation event, plus lead_status_changed when its status actually changed (origin bulk), in the same transaction, from which the automation worker later starts runs of the enabled rules with a matching trigger; and, once the id, status and role checks have passed, an audit row is written to the bulk operation log and marked completed or failed.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Request: application/json · Response: application/json

Lead IDs and the status to set

PUT/leads/{id}

Update a lead

Partial update. Roles: admin (any lead) and sales (only the leads it owns). Every field is optional and an empty string counts as absent, so a text field cannot be cleared here; the email-or-phone rule of create is not re-checked. Only an admin may send owner_id (any other role gets 403, even for its own id); the new owner is not checked against the users table by the handler. company_id: absent keeps the link, 0 clears it, another value must name a live company (400 INVALID_REFERENCE). status converted is refused (400 VALIDATION_ERROR) unless the lead is already converted. The lead is re-read under a row lock in the writing transaction, so the update never overwrites a concurrent conversion with a stale copy.

Side effect: lead_updated is recorded in the same transaction, plus lead_status_changed (with the previous and the new status) when the status changes.

The response is the updated lead; owner is not loaded here (all its fields are zero) and company_record is omitted.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Request: application/json · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
idrequiredpathinteger

Lead ID

requestrequiredbodyhandler.UpdateLeadRequest

Fields to change; empty strings and absent fields are left unchanged

StatusBodyDescription
200utils.APIResponse with data: models.Lead

The updated lead

400utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

BAD_REQUEST: id is not an unsigned integer ('Invalid lead ID') or notes over 65535 bytes. VALIDATION_ERROR (message 'Validation failed'): a binding rule failed; error.details maps the Go field name (for example FirstName) to a message. BAD_REQUEST: the body is not valid JSON for the schema ('Invalid request format'). VALIDATION_ERROR: status converted for a lead that is not converted, with error.details {'field': 'status'}. INVALID_REFERENCE: company_id names no live company

401utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

UNAUTHORIZED: the Authorization header is missing or is neither Bearer <jwt> nor ApiKey <key> ('Missing or invalid authorization header'), or the credential is invalid, expired or belongs to a deactivated account ('Invalid credentials')

DELETE/leads/{id}

Delete (erase) a lead

GDPR erasure, irreversible. Roles: admin (any lead) and sales (only the leads it owns, and only leads that were never converted). The lead's personal fields are overwritten in place (the email becomes a random address in the reserved .invalid domain; names, phone, company, position, external id and notes are cleared), linked form submissions are scrubbed, and the row is then soft-deleted, all in one transaction. A converted lead is the same person as the customer it became, so erasing it also erases that customer, the customer's own customer-role login (credentials included) and every other lead converted into it; staff accounts, owners and assignees are never erased. company_id and deal links stay. A sales user asking to delete a converted lead gets 403 and nothing changes.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
idrequiredpathinteger

Lead ID

StatusBodyDescription
204no body

Erased; no body

400utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

BAD_REQUEST: id is not an unsigned integer ('Invalid lead ID')

401utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

UNAUTHORIZED: the Authorization header is missing or is neither Bearer <jwt> nor ApiKey <key> ('Missing or invalid authorization header'), or the credential is invalid, expired or belongs to a deactivated account ('Invalid credentials')

403utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

FORBIDDEN 'Insufficient permissions': the caller is not admin or sales; a sales user asked for another owner's lead ('You can only delete your own leads'), or for a converted lead ('Only an admin can delete a converted lead: it also erases the customer it became')

404utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

NOT_FOUND: no live lead has this id ('Lead not found')

429utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

INTERNAL_ERROR: the erasure failed (nothing is written when it failed before the commit; a failed COMMIT is ambiguous and the erasure may have been applied) or the lead vanished between the lookup and the erasure

POST/leads/{id}/convert

Convert a lead into a customer

Create a customer from the lead and mark the lead converted, in one transaction. Roles: admin (any lead) and sales (only the leads it owns).

The customer gets the lead's first_name, last_name, email and phone; company is company_name or, when that is empty, the lead's free-text company; website (trimmed, at most 255 characters, an http or https URL with a host), address and notes come from the body. The lead's company_id is copied when it still names a live company (a link to a deleted company is dropped and the conversion succeeds). With leads.conversion.auto_assign_owner on, the lead's owner becomes the customer's assigned_to_id (never user_id).

Refusals, before anything is written: a lead already converted (status converted or a customer_id set) is 400 BAD_REQUEST; the lead's status must be one of leads.conversion.allowed_statuses (re-checked under a row lock) and notes must not be blank when leads.conversion.require_notes is on, both 400 VALIDATION_ERROR; a lead without an email is 400 BAD_REQUEST (every customer needs one); an email that already belongs to a customer is 409. A failure to read the conversion settings is a 500. Deadlocks are retried up to three times.

Side effect: lead_updated and lead_status_changed (to converted, origin conversion) are recorded in the same transaction. No customer_created event exists.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Request: application/json · Response: application/json

Lead ID

The new customer

VALIDATION_ERROR: a conversion setting refused it; error.details is {'field': 'status', 'status': <lead status>, 'allowed_statuses': [...]} or {'field': 'notes'}

customers

Customers, CSV export, linked login, their tickets.

GET/customers

List customers

Admin, sales and support (checked in the handler; customer gets 403). All three roles see the same unfiltered set of live customers; there is no ownership narrowing. data holds customers and total; meta repeats the pagination (page, per_page, total, total_pages, each omitted when 0). Without sort_by the rows come in ascending id order; with an allowed sort_by they are ordered by that column, then id, in the requested direction. search matches a substring (SQL LIKE) of first_name, last_name, email, company, phone or notes and combines with the sort. The embedded user (the customer's login) is loaded only for the default listing without search and sort_by.

Pagination: limit defaults to 20 and is capped at 100 (a missing, non-numeric or non-positive value gives 20); offset defaults to 0; page, when a positive integer, overrides offset with (page - 1) * limit. An unknown sort_by is ignored rather than rejected.

GET/customers/export

Export customers as CSV (admin)

Admin only (route guard, repeated in the handler), deliberately narrower than the list, which sales and support can read: one request egresses the personal data of the whole customer base, so data minimisation keeps it from the roles that work one record at a time.

The success body is NOT the JSON envelope: it is the raw CSV, Content-Type text/csv; charset=utf-8 and Content-Disposition attachment; filename=customers-export.csv. Errors raised before the file starts (401, 403, 429, 500) still use the JSON envelope. Every row is read before the first byte is written, so a database failure is a JSON 500, never a truncated file.

Columns, in this order: id, first_name, last_name, email, phone, company, address, notes, assigned_to_id, created_at, updated_at, website. Timestamps are RFC 3339; an unassigned customer has an empty assigned_to_id. A text cell starting with =, @, a tab or a carriage return, or with + or - followed by anything other than a plain number, is prefixed with an apostrophe so spreadsheets do not run it as a formula.

Not paginated: every live customer matching search is included; erased customers never are. Without sort_by the rows come in ascending id order; with an allowed sort_by they are ordered by that column, then id, in the requested direction.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Response: text/csv, application/json

searchquerystring

Substring matched against first_name, last_name, email, company, phone and notes

sort_byquerystring

Sort column; any other value is ignored (ascending id order)

one of: "created_at", "updated_at", "first_name", "last_name", "email", "company"
sort_orderquerystring

Sort direction, used only with sort_by; any other value falls back to asc

one of: "asc", "desc"; default "asc"
StatusBodyDescription
200string

The CSV file (raw body, not the JSON envelope)

DELETE/customers/{id}

Erase a customer (irreversible)

Admin only (route guard, repeated in the handler). A GDPR Article 17 erasure, not an archive: in one transaction every personal field (names, email, phone, company, website, position, postal address, notes) is overwritten in place, the email becoming an unrelated random address in the reserved .invalid domain, and the row is soft-deleted so references from tickets, tasks and deals still resolve. The same person is erased everywhere in that transaction: every lead converted into the customer (with its linked form submissions scrubbed) and, when user_id names a customer-role account, that login (credentials purged) and its other customer records. A staff account in user_id, the account manager (assigned_to_id), company_id and deals are left as they are. Any failure before the commit rolls the whole erasure back. A failed COMMIT is the exception: its outcome is ambiguous (the erasure may have been applied), so read the record again before retrying.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
idrequiredpathinteger

Customer ID

StatusBodyDescription
204no body

Erased; no body

400utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

BAD_REQUEST: Invalid customer ID

401utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

UNAUTHORIZED: missing or malformed Authorization header, or an invalid, expired or revoked credential, or a deactivated or erased account

403utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

FORBIDDEN: the caller is not an admin (Insufficient permissions)

404utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

NOT_FOUND: no live customer with this id (an already erased customer included)

429utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

TOO_MANY_REQUESTS: authenticated tier exceeded (120 per minute, burst 30, per client IP); details.retry_after is 60s and no Retry-After header is sent

500utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

INTERNAL_ERROR: the erasure failed; before the commit it was rolled back and nothing was erased, while a failed COMMIT is ambiguous (the erasure may have been applied)

POST/customers/{id}/assign

Assign a customer to an account manager

Admin and sales (route guard, repeated in the handler); sales may assign any customer, not only its own. Sets assigned_to_id to user_id and returns the customer (with its login and company embedded; assigned_to itself is not embedded). The target must exist, be active and hold the admin or sales role: support and customer accounts are refused, since customer ownership is a sales function and handing a customer account a book of other people's records would be a data-protection incident.

A missing customer or user is 404; an existing user that is deactivated or holds the wrong role is 400, because the request named a real account and was refused on its merits.

companies

Companies (organisations) and their linked records.

GET/companies

List companies

One page of live companies, each with its owner and its customer_count, lead_count and deal_count (counted over all live records, whoever owns them). Roles: admin, sales and support.

search is a substring match on name, domain, industry and city (case-insensitive on MySQL/MariaDB with their default collations, and on SQLite for ASCII letters only). sort_by must be one of the listed columns (any other value is 400) and the id breaks ties in the same direction. Without sort_by the order is created_at descending and sort_order is ignored; with sort_by, a sort_order other than asc or desc (case-insensitive) becomes desc.

data is the array of companies; meta carries request_id, page (offset / limit + 1, rounded down), per_page and, when not zero, total and total_pages (both are omitted for an empty result).

POST/companies

Create a company

Create a company. Roles: admin and sales.

Validation, in order: binding (name required, employee_range from its set), column widths (name 200, domain 255, website 255, industry 100, employee_range 20, phone 50, address 255, city, state and country 100, postal_code 20 characters; notes 65535 bytes), then the service: the trimmed name must not be empty, the domain is normalised and must be a bare host, the website must be an http or https URL, the owner must be a live user, and the domain must be free among live companies.

Owner: a sales user may only name itself and becomes the owner when owner_id is absent or 0; an admin may name any live user, or leave the company unowned by omitting it. The owner may be any live account; its role is not checked.

The response is the stored company re-read with its owner and counts (all 0 for a new company).

PUT/companies/{id}

Replace a company

Replace a company's fields. Roles: admin and sales; a sales user may update any company, not only the ones it owns. The body is the new state of every text field: a field left out is stored empty, which is how a domain or a note is cleared. The validation of create applies; the company may keep its own domain. owner_id: absent keeps the owner, 0 clears it (admin only), another id sets it (sales: only its own id). The owner, also a kept one, must still be a live user: a company whose stored owner account was deleted is refused with 400 INVALID_REFERENCE until owner_id is changed.

The response is the company re-read with its owner and counts.

GET/deals

List deals

One page of live deals with owner, company, customer and lead loaded. Roles: admin (every deal) and sales (only the deals it owns, whatever owner_id says; the filter is applied in the query, so meta.total counts only those). lead_id and lead appear only when the caller may see the linked lead (admins every lead, sales users the leads they own), decided on every read.

Without sort_by the order is created_at descending and sort_order is ignored; with sort_by, a sort_order other than asc or desc becomes desc.

data is the array of deals; meta carries request_id, page (offset / limit + 1, rounded down), per_page and, when not zero, total and total_pages (both are omitted for an empty result).

POST/deals

Create a deal

Create a deal. Roles: admin and sales.

Validation, in order: binding (title required, stage from its set, amount_cents 0 to 10^12, currency three upper-case letters, probability 0 to 100, owner_id at least 1); title 200, lost_reason 255 and source 100 characters and notes 65535 bytes; expected_close_date YYYY-MM-DD; then the service (blank title after trimming) and the links: owner_id, company_id, customer_id and lead_id must name live rows, and lead_id a lead the caller may see (a hidden lead is reported like a missing one).

Defaults and stage rules: stage qualification; currency from the deals.default_currency setting (EUR as shipped; an unusable setting falls back to EUR); probability from the stage (qualification 10, proposal 40, negotiation 70), won always 100 and lost always 0; closed_at set when the stage is won or lost; lost_reason kept only when lost. owner_id defaults to the caller; a sales user may only name itself.

Side effects, in one transaction: the first stage-history row (from_stage null, to_stage the initial stage) and the deal_created automation event.

The response is the deal re-read with owner, company, customer and lead loaded; lead_id and lead appear only when the caller may see the linked lead (admins every lead, sales users the leads they own), decided on every read.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Request: application/json · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
requestrequiredbodyhandler.DealRequest

The deal to create

StatusBodyDescription
201utils.APIResponse with data: models.Deal

Deal created

400utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

BAD_REQUEST: the body is not valid JSON for the schema ('Invalid request format'). BAD_REQUEST: a value longer than its column, a malformed expected_close_date, or a blank title. INVALID_REFERENCE: owner_id, company_id, customer_id or lead_id names no live row, or lead_id names a lead the caller may not see

401utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

UNAUTHORIZED: the Authorization header is missing or is neither Bearer <jwt> nor ApiKey <key> ('Missing or invalid authorization header'), or the credential is invalid, expired or belongs to a deactivated account ('Invalid credentials')

GET/deals/pipeline

Deal pipeline by stage

The live deals aggregated per stage and currency. Roles: admin and sales; a sales user always gets its own deals only, whatever owner_id says.

data.stages lists all five stages in pipeline order (qualification, proposal, negotiation, won, lost), each present even when empty (count 0, totals []). count is the number of deals in the stage; totals holds one entry per currency, sorted by currency code; amounts in different currencies are never added together. amount_cents is the sum of the deals' amounts; weighted_cents is round_half_up(sum of amount_cents x probability / 100), integer arithmetic, rounded once on each stage-and-currency total. Soft-deleted deals are not counted. owner_id (admin) and company_id narrow the aggregate; an id that matches nothing yields empty stages.

PUT/deals/{id}

Replace a deal

Replace a deal's fields. Roles: admin (any deal) and sales (only the deals it owns).

The body is the new state of title, amount_cents (absent means 0), expected_close_date, lost_reason, source and notes: a field left out is stored empty, 0 or cleared. stage, currency, owner_id absent keep the stored value. company_id, customer_id and lead_id absent keep the stored link, 0 clears it, another id sets it (a live row, else 400 INVALID_REFERENCE). Links are checked only when they change, so a deal keeps links to erased records and stays editable; a lead_id that is sent is always checked, even when unchanged, and must name a lead the caller may see; a stored lead link the caller may not see is kept unchecked when lead_id is absent. owner_id: a sales user may only send its own id; an admin any live user (checked only when it changes).

A stage different from the stored one follows the rules of POST /deals/{id}/stage (probability defaults to the new stage's unless sent, won 100, lost 0, closed_at set or cleared, lost_reason kept only on lost) and writes a stage-history row. The same stage writes no history row: closed_at stays, probability changes only when sent (still pinned for won and lost), and lost_reason survives only on a lost deal.

Side effects, in one transaction: the deal_updated automation event, plus deal_stage_changed and the history row when the stage changes.

The response is the deal re-read with its associations; lead_id and lead appear only when the caller may see the linked lead (admins every lead, sales users the leads they own), decided on every read.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Request: application/json · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
idrequiredpathinteger

Deal ID

requestrequiredbodyhandler.DealRequest

The new state of the deal

StatusBodyDescription
200utils.APIResponse with data: models.Deal

The updated deal

POST/deals/{id}/stage

Move a deal to another stage

Move a deal to a stage. Roles: admin (any deal) and sales (only the deals it owns). probability defaults to the new stage's (qualification 10, proposal 40, negotiation 70) unless sent; won is always 100 and lost always 0. Entering won or lost sets closed_at to now (also on won to lost); entering an open stage clears it. lost_reason is stored (trimmed) when the new stage is lost, absent keeping the reason already stored, and cleared otherwise. Asking for the stage the deal is already in is a 200 that writes nothing (no history row, no event) and ignores probability and lost_reason.

Side effects, in one transaction: one stage-history row and the deal_stage_changed automation event (no deal_updated event).

The response is the deal re-read with its associations; lead_id and lead appear only when the caller may see the linked lead (admins every lead, sales users the leads they own), decided on every read.

POST/products

Create a product

Create a catalogue product. Role: admin. name is required (trimmed, 1 to 200 characters); sku is optional (trimmed, at most 64 characters, indexed but not unique); description at most 65535 bytes. unit_price_cents is required, an integer in minor units from 0 to 10^12. currency is three upper-case letters and defaults to the deals.default_currency setting (EUR as shipped; an unusable setting falls back to EUR). is_active defaults to true. POST/quotes

Create a quote

Create a draft quote. Roles: admin and sales.

Rules: title required (trimmed, 1 to 200 characters); notes at most 65535 bytes; valid_until YYYY-MM-DD; currency three upper-case letters, defaulting to the deals.default_currency setting (EUR as shipped). At least one of deal_id and customer_id is required. With deal_id: the deal must be live (else 400 INVALID_REFERENCE), owned by the caller when the caller is sales (403), and neither won nor lost (409); without customer_id the deal's customer is used, possibly none (such a quote cannot be emailed); a customer_id different from the deal's customer is 400 VALIDATION_ERROR. customer_id must name a live customer. owner_id defaults to the caller; a sales user may only name itself; the owner must be a live user.

The quote starts as a draft with no lines and totals of 0, whatever else was sent; its first status-history row (from_status null, to_status draft) is written in the same transaction. No automation event is recorded for quotes.

The response is the quote re-read with its number, lines and the customer, deal and owner summaries.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Request: application/json · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
requestrequiredbodyhandler.QuoteRequest

The quote to create

StatusBodyDescription
201utils.APIResponse with data: models.QuoteJSON

Quote created

400utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

VALIDATION_ERROR: a binding rule failed (message 'Validation failed', error.details maps the Go field name to a message), or a rule checked by the handler or the service failed (the message names it, no details). BAD_REQUEST: the body is not valid JSON for the schema ('Invalid request format'): title over 200 characters or blank, notes over 65535 bytes, a malformed valid_until, neither deal_id nor customer_id, or a customer_id that is not the deal's customer. INVALID_REFERENCE: deal_id, customer_id or owner_id names no live row

401utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

UNAUTHORIZED: the Authorization header is missing or is neither Bearer <jwt> nor ApiKey <key> ('Missing or invalid authorization header'), or the credential is invalid, expired or belongs to a deactivated account ('Invalid credentials')

403utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

FORBIDDEN 'Insufficient permissions': the caller is not admin or sales; a sales user named another owner ('You can only assign quotes to yourself') or quoted a deal it does not own ('You can only work on your own deals and quotes')

CONFLICT: the deal is won or lost

429utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

TOO_MANY_REQUESTS: more than 120 requests per minute (burst 30) from this client IP, one bucket shared by every authenticated endpoint; error.details.retry_after is '60s' and no Retry-After or X-RateLimit header is sent

PUT/quotes/{id}

Update a draft quote

Partial update of a draft quote. Roles: admin (any quote) and sales (only the quotes it owns). Every absent field keeps its stored value; deal_id is not editable. valid_until takes YYYY-MM-DD, or null or an empty string to clear it. customer_id, when it changes, must name a live customer (400 INVALID_REFERENCE) and, when the quote's deal has a customer, be that customer (400 VALIDATION_ERROR). currency may change only while the quote has no lines (409 otherwise). owner_id: a sales user may only name itself; an admin any live user (checked when it changes). The stored quote is re-read under a row lock and written only while it is still a draft.

The response is the quote re-read with its lines and summaries.

POST/quotes/{id}/email

Email a quote to its customer

Send the quote as plain text (number, title, currency, validity, every line, subtotal, discount, total and notes) to the linked customer's current email address. Roles: admin (any quote) and sales (only the quotes it owns). No request body. The subject is 'Quote Q-000042 from GopherCRM' for quote 42.

Only a sent or accepted quote can be emailed; a quote without a customer, whose customer was erased, or whose customer has no email address is refused; all 409 and no mail is sent. No transaction is held during the delivery; afterwards last_emailed_at is stamped by a conditional update that matches only while the quote is still sent or accepted and no revision back to draft committed since the request started (even one followed by a new send); otherwise 409 with last_emailed_at unchanged (the mail has then already gone out). With SMTP_HOST unset the server only logs the message and the request succeeds. A delivery failure is 502 MAIL_DELIVERY_FAILED. The status does not change.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Response: application/json

idrequiredpathinteger

Quote ID

StatusBodyDescription
200utils.APIResponse with data: models.QuoteJSON

The quote with last_emailed_at set

FORBIDDEN 'Insufficient permissions': the caller is not admin or sales; or FORBIDDEN 'You can only view your own quotes': a sales user addressed a quote it does not own

404utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

NOT_FOUND: no live quote has this id ('Quote not found')

409utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

POST/quotes/{id}/lines

Add a line to a draft quote

Add a line priced from a product. Roles: admin (any quote) and sales (only the quotes it owns). The product must be live and active (400 INVALID_REFERENCE otherwise) and priced in the quote's currency (400 VALIDATION_ERROR). The product's name and price are copied onto the line unless description (when not blank) or unit_price_cents override them; a later change to the product never alters the line. The new line's position is the highest position plus one. quantity is 1 to 1000000, discount_bp 0 to 10000 (basis points: 1250 is 12.5 percent), unit_price_cents 0 to 10^12; quantity x unit_price_cents and the resulting quote total may not exceed 10^12, and a quote holds at most 100 lines (all 400 VALIDATION_ERROR, nothing written).

One transaction: a locking read of the quote (SELECT ... FOR UPDATE on MySQL/MariaDB), the draft check, the insert, then the totals recomputed from the lines and written only while the quote is still a draft.

The response is the whole quote with its lines and recomputed totals.

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Request: application/json · Response: application/json

POST/quotes/{id}/status

Move a quote to another status

Change the status. Roles: admin (any quote) and sales (only the quotes it owns). The allowed moves are draft to sent, sent to accepted, sent to rejected, sent to expired and sent back to draft; accepted, rejected and expired are final. Any other pair, the same status again included, is 400 INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION and writes nothing.

One transaction: a locking read of the quote, the transition check, the acceptance check (accepted is 409 once valid_until lies before today's date in the server's zone), a conditional claim (WHERE status = the stored status; a lost race is 400 INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION), for draft to sent a locked count of the lines (none rolls everything back with 409), and one status-history row.

Timestamps: sent sets sent_at and clears closed_at; accepted, rejected and expired set closed_at; sent to draft clears both. Accepting a quote does not change its deal, and no email is sent (see POST /quotes/{id}/email).

Authentication: BearerAuth (Authorization header), ApiKeyAuth (Authorization header) · Request: application/json · Response: application/json

ParameterInTypeDescription
idrequiredpathinteger

Quote ID

requestrequiredbodyhandler.QuoteStatusRequest

Target status

StatusBodyDescription
200utils.APIResponse with data: models.QuoteJSON

The quote after the move

400utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

VALIDATION_ERROR (message 'Validation failed'): a binding rule failed; error.details maps the Go field name (for example FirstName) to a message. BAD_REQUEST: the body is not valid JSON for the schema ('Invalid request format') (status missing or not one of the five). INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION: the move is not allowed from the current status, or another transition won the race

401utils.APIResponse with error: utils.APIError

UNAUTHORIZED: the Authorization header is missing or is neither Bearer <jwt> nor ApiKey <key> ('Missing or invalid authorization header'), or the credential is invalid, expired or belongs to a deactivated account ('Invalid credentials')

GET/tickets

List tickets

One page of tickets with their customer and assignee. Roles: admin and sales see every ticket; support sees only tickets assigned to them (unassigned tickets and other people's are never listed), a scope applied in the query before offset and limit so the tickets, total and meta all reflect it and no parameter can widen it; customers get 403. Precedence: for support, the assignee scope with optional search and sort; otherwise search (with optional sort), then sort_by, then the plain list. search is a substring match (SQL LIKE) on title, description and resolution. An unknown sort_by is ignored (ascending id) and any sort_order other than asc or desc falls back to asc. Pagination: page (1-based) overrides offset when greater than 0; limit defaults to 20 and is clamped to 100. Soft-deleted tickets are never listed; comments are not included.

Update the status of several tickets at once

Set the same status on up to 100 tickets in one all-or-nothing transaction. Roles: admin may update any ticket, support only tickets assigned to them; sales (read-only on tickets) and customers get 403 before the body is read. Repeated ids are collapsed. If any listed ticket is missing (404, error.details.missing_ids), not assigned to a support caller (403, error.details.forbidden_ids) or closed while the target status is not closed (400, error.details.closed_ids), nothing is written; the checks report in that order. No automation event is recorded; once the id, status and role checks have passed, an audit row is written to the bulk operation log and marked completed or failed.
